cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(black_scholes_benchmark)

=== black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

The environment has Open MPI, OpenMP-capable compilers, CUDA 12, and RTX 3090 devices. +__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                         options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
     }
+    cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+    cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);

 // Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set

+void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t firstOption,
+                     const size_t numOptions) {
     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
     options.resize(numOptions);
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long local = 0; local < static_cast<long long>(numOptions); ++local) {
+        const size_t i = firstOption + static_cast<size_t>(local);
         // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
         const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
         options[i] = base;
         
         // Add some variation for larger datasets
         const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
-        options[i].spot *= factor;
-        options[i].strike *= factor;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].spot *= factor;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].strike *= factor;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
+    }
+}
+
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                         options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
     }
+    cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+    cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
 }

+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+    }
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
+}
+

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

    98	
    99	// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
   100	void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t firstOption,
   101	                     const size_t numOptions) {
   102	    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
   103	    options.resize(numOptions);
   104	
   105	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   106	    for (long long local = 0; local < static_cast<long long>(numOptions); ++local) {
   107	        const size_t i = firstOption + static_cast<size_t>(local);
   108	        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
   109	        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
   110	        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)] = base;
   111	        
   112	        // Add some variation for larger datasets
   113	        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
   114	        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].spot *= factor;
   115	        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].strike *= factor;
   116	    }
   117	}
   118	
   119	__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
   120	                                   const size_t count) {
   121	    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   122	    if (index < count) {
   123	        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
   124	    }
   125	}
   126	
   127	void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
   128	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
   129	        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
   130	                cudaGetErrorString(status));
   131	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   132	    }
   133	}
   134	
   135	void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
   136	                const int rank, const int localRank) {
   137	    int deviceCount = 0;
   138	    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
   139	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   140	        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
   141	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   142	    }
   143	    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
   144	
   145	    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
   146	    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
   147	    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
   148	    if (!options.empty()) {
   149	        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
   150	        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
   151	                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
   152	        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   153	                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
   154	        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
   155	        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
   156	        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
   157	        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
   158	        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
   159	                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   160	                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
   161	    }
   162	        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
   163	        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
   164	    }
   165	}
   166	
   167	bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
   168	                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
   169	    bool allPassed = true;
   170	    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
   171	    
   172	    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
   173	    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
   174	        const double computed = results[i];
   175	        const double expected = options[i].value;
   176	        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
   177	        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
   178	        
   179	        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
   180	               i, computed, expected, relError);
   181	        
   182	        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
   183	        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
   184	            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
   185	            allPassed = false;
   186	        }
   187	    }
   188	    
   189	    return allPassed;
   190	}
   191	
   192	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   193	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   194	    printf("Options:\n");
   195	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
   196	    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
   197	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   198	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   199	}

+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
     }
 }
 
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+    }
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
+}
+

-

+        const size_t i = firstOption + static_cast<size_t>(local);

+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
     }
 }
 
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+    }
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
+}
+

+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].strike *= factor;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
     }
 }
 
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
+}

The first build exposed only one code-quality issue: helper math functions were host-only while called from the device-qualified pricing routine. The executable still linked, but I’m tightening those annotations and then testing single-rank and multi-rank GPU execution, including uneven partitioning.

 // Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
-void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
+void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t firstOption,

-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+

+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
     }
 }
 
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
+}
+

+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
+}

+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
+    }
+}
+
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
 }

Single-rank and two-rank runs both completed on the GPU and preserved the original validation behavior. I noticed rank 0’s validation set was only its local partition in the multi-rank case, so I corrected that to regenerate the full deterministic reference set on rank 0; I’m doing the final rebuild and edge-case checks now.

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}

+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,

+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }

-    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long local = 0; local < static_cast<long long>(numOptions); ++local) {
+        const size_t i = firstOption + static_cast<size_t>(local);
         // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
         const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
-        options[i] = base;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)] = base;
         
         // Add some variation for larger datasets
         const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
-        options[i].spot *= factor;
-        options[i].strike *= factor;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].spot *= factor;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].strike *= factor;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
+    }
+}
+
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
 }

-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (long long local = 0; local < static_cast<long long>(numOptions); ++local) {
+        const size_t i = firstOption + static_cast<size_t>(local);
         // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
         const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
-        options[i] = base;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)] = base;
         
         // Add some variation for larger datasets
         const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
-        options[i].spot *= factor;
-        options[i].strike *= factor;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].spot *= factor;
+        options[static_cast<size_t>(local)].strike *= factor;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void priceOptionsKernel(const OptionInput* options, double* results,
+                                   const size_t count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (index < count) {
+        results[index] = blackScholes(options[index]);
+    }
+}
+
+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void priceOnGpu(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, std::vector<double>& results,
+                const int rank, const int localRank) {
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: no CUDA device is available\n", rank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
     }
+    cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "cudaSetDevice", rank);
+
+    OptionInput* deviceOptions = nullptr;
+    double* deviceResults = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = options.size() * sizeof(OptionInput);
+    if (!options.empty()) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceOptions), bytes), "cudaMalloc(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double)), "cudaMalloc(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceOptions, options.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(options)", rank);
+        constexpr unsigned threads = 256;
+        const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>((options.size() + threads - 1) / threads);
+        priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, threads>>>(deviceOptions, deviceResults, options.size());
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptionsKernel", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                             options.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "cudaMemcpy(results)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceOptions), "cudaFree(options)", rank);
+        cudaCheck(cudaFree(deviceResults), "cudaFree(results)", rank);
+    }
 }

codex
Implemented the hybrid accelerator version in the existing files:

- MPI partitions options across ranks and gathers ordered results.
- OpenMP parallelizes host-side option generation.
- CUDA kernel prices options on rank-local GPUs.
- GPU assignment uses local MPI rank.
- CMake now requires and links MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA while preserving executable name `black_scholes`.
- Multi-rank validation and uneven partitioning are handled correctly.

Verified with:

- CUDA build using CMake/NVCC.
- 1 MPI rank, 17 options, validation.
- 2 MPI ranks, 17 options, validation.
- 4 MPI ranks, 3 options, validation and result output.
